# MPC 3.10.1 Lua API dump (LaunchpadProMK3)

Snapshot of the `mpc` and `dev` Lua tables as seen by a controller script on
MPC 3.10.1.8 (build 38fdde70994), MPC Live III Gen2, captured 2026-11-17.

Generated by a temporary debug block in `script/LaunchpadProMK3.lua` that walks
`mpc` and `dev` recursively (depth 3) and logs every key, type, and userdata
`.value`. The block was removed after capture; this file is the reference.

Format: `<table> <key>=<type>(<value>),...` — one line per table, chunked at
1500 chars. `userdata(...)` is the property's `.value`; `function` is a callable;
`table` is a sub-table (dumped on its own line).

Note: `normalTracks` is indexed 0..127 (128 slots allocated whatever the
project holds); empty slots have `Name=userdata()`, `Colour=userdata(0)`,
`Volume=userdata(0.0)`.
